Haiku 4.5
Ótimo! Esse package.json é profissional e usa pnpm (gerenciador melhor que npm).

Vou analisar:

🔍 O QUE ESSE package.json FAZ:
Copiar
"dev": "export NODE_ENV=development && pnpm run build && pnpm run start"
✅ Compila com build.mjs
✅ Depois executa o arquivo compilado

Copiar
"build": "node ./build.mjs"
✅ Usa o script que você mostrou antes (esbuild)

Copiar
"start": "node --enable-source-maps ./dist/index.mjs"
✅ Executa o arquivo compilado

⚠️ PROBLEMA:
Esse package.json é de um workspace (projeto monorepo):

Copiar
"@workspace/api-zod": "workspace:*",
"@workspace/db": "workspace:*"
Isso significa que depende de outros projetos na mesma pasta!

🎯 VOCÊ TEM 2 PROJETOS?
Responda:

Qual é a estrutura da pasta?

Copiar
pasta-principal/
├── api-server/          ← Seu projeto?
├── api-zod/             ← Outro projeto?
├── db/                  ← Outro projeto?
└── pnpm-workspace.yaml
Ou é só um projeto isolado?

Qual é o erro quando tenta rodar?

🚀 SE FOR UM PROJETO ISOLADO:
Crie este package.json simples:

Copiar
{
  "name": "meu-app-juridico",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "type": "module",
  "scripts": {
    "dev": "node ./src/index.js",
    "build": "node ./build.mjs",
    "start": "node ./dist/index.mjs"
  },
  "dependencies": {
    "express": "^5",
    "cors": "^2",
    "cookie-parser": "^1.4.7",
    "pino": "^9",
    "pino-http": "^10",
    "ws": "^8.20.0"
  },
  "devDependencies": {
    "esbuild": "^0.27.3",
    "esbuild-plugin-pino": "^2.3.3"
  }
}

## STACK TECNOLOGICO DETECTADO

- **Frontend:** React, TypeScript

---

## ROTAS DA API (endpoints detectados automaticamente)

```
USE    /api  (em api-server/src/app.ts)
POST   /ai/process  (em api-server/src/routes/aiProcess.ts)
POST   /code-assistant  (em api-server/src/routes/aiProcess.ts)
GET    /gh-proxy/zipball  (em api-server/src/routes/ghProxy.ts)
GET    /gh-proxy/repo  (em api-server/src/routes/ghProxy.ts)
GET    /healthz  (em api-server/src/routes/health.ts)
GET    /config  (em api-server/src/routes/index.ts)
POST   /site-extract  (em api-server/src/routes/siteExtract.ts)
GET    /site-extract/proxy  (em api-server/src/routes/siteExtract.ts)
GET    /api/items  (em code-editor/src/lib/templates.ts)
GET    /api/items/:id  (em code-editor/src/lib/templates.ts)
POST   /api/items  (em code-editor/src/lib/templates.ts)
GET    /api/health  (em code-editor/src/lib/templates.ts)
USE    /api/auth  (em code-editor/src/lib/templates.ts)
USE    /api/usuarios  (em code-editor/src/lib/templates.ts)
POST   /register  (em code-editor/src/lib/templates.ts)
POST   /login  (em code-editor/src/lib/templates.ts)
GET    /perfil  (em code-editor/src/lib/templates.ts)
```

---

## VARIAVEIS DE AMBIENTE NECESSARIAS

Crie um arquivo `.env` na raiz com estas variaveis:

```env
LOG_LEVEL=seu_valor_aqui
PORT=seu_valor_aqui
BASE_PATH=seu_valor_aqui
REPL_ID=seu_valor_aqui
ALLOWED_ORIGINS=seu_valor_aqui
JWT_SECRET=seu_valor_aqui
JWT_EXPIRES_IN=seu_valor_aqui
DATABASE_URL=seu_valor_aqui
EXPO_PUBLIC_DOMAIN=seu_valor_aqui
REPLIT_INTERNAL_APP_DOMAIN=seu_valor_aqui
REPLIT_DEV_DOMAIN=seu_valor_aqui
EXPO_PUBLIC_REPL_ID=seu_valor_aqui
```

---

## ARQUIVOS PRINCIPAIS

- `api-server/src/app.ts` — Ponto de entrada do backend
- `api-server/src/index.ts` — Ponto de entrada do backend
- `api-server/src/routes/index.ts` — Ponto de entrada do backend
- `apk-builder/dist-apk/index.html` — Arquivo principal
- `apk-builder/dist-standalone/index.html` — Arquivo principal
- `apk-builder/index.html` — Arquivo principal
- `apk-builder/src/App.tsx` — Componente raiz do frontend
- `apk-builder/src/main.tsx` — Arquivo principal
- `assistente-juridico/dist-apk/index.html` — Arquivo principal
- `assistente-juridico/dist-standalone/index.html` — Arquivo principal

---

## GUIA COMPLETO — O QUE CADA PARTE DO PROJETO FAZ

> Esta secao explica, em linguagem simples, o que e para que serve cada pasta e cada arquivo.

### 📁 Raiz do Projeto (pasta principal)
> Arquivos de configuracao e pontos de entrada ficam aqui.

**`extrator.html`** _(920 linhas)_
Arquivo HTML — parte do projeto.

---

### 📁 `api-server/`
> Pasta 'api-server' — agrupamento de arquivos relacionados.

**`build.mjs`** _(127 linhas)_
Arquivo MJS — parte do projeto.

**`package.json`** _(38 linhas)_
Registro de dependencias e scripts do projeto. Aqui ficam os comandos (npm run dev, npm start) e os pacotes instalados.

**`tsconfig.json`** _(18 linhas)_
Configuracao do TypeScript. Diz para o computador como interpretar o codigo .ts e .tsx.

---

### 📁 `apk-builder/`
> Pasta 'apk-builder' — agrupamento de arquivos relacionados.

**`components.json`** _(20 linhas)_
Arquivo de dados ou configuracao no formato JSON (chave: valor).

**`index.html`** _(40 linhas)_
Pagina HTML raiz do projeto. E o ponto de entrada que o browser carrega primeiro.

**`package.json`** _(91 linhas)_
Registro de dependencias e scripts do projeto. Aqui ficam os comandos (npm run dev, npm start) e os pacotes instalados.

**`tsconfig.json`** _(23 linhas)_
Configuracao do TypeScript. Diz para o computador como interpretar o codigo .ts e .tsx.

**`vite.config.apk.ts`** _(22 linhas)_

"use strict";
var http  = require("http");
var os    = require("os");
var cp    = require("child_process");
var buf   = require("buffer");

// Incluir assets embutidos
var EMBEDDED = require("./server_assets.js");

var MIME = {
  "html":"text/html; charset=utf-8",
  "js":"application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  "css":"text/css; charset=utf-8",
  "json":"application/json",
  "svg":"image/svg+xml",
  "png":"image/png",
  "jpg":"image/jpeg","jpeg":"image/jpeg",
  "ico":"image/x-icon"
};

var PORT = 18633;

function serve(req, res) {
  var url = (req.url || "/").split("?")[0];
  var key = url === "/" ? "index.html" : url.replace(/^\//, "");
  var b64  = EMBEDDED[key] || EMBEDDED["index.html"];
  if (!b64) { res.writeHead(404); res.end("not found"); return; }
  var ext  = key.lastIndexOf(".") > -1 ? key.slice(key.lastIndexOf(".")+1).toLowerCase() : "";
  var mime = MIME[ext] || "application/octet-stream";
  var data = buf.Buffer.from(b64, "base64");
  res.writeHead(200, {"Content-Type": mime, "Cache-Control":"no-store", "Content-Length": data.length});
  res.end(data);
}

var server = http.createServer(serve);
server.on("error", function(e) {
  if (e.code === "EADDRINUSE") {
    console.log("\n  [ERRO] Porta " + PORT + " ja esta em uso.");
    console.log("  Feche a outra janela do SK Code Editor e tente novamente.\n");
  } else {
    console.log("\n  [ERRO] " + e.message + "\n");
  }
  console.log("  Pressione ENTER para fechar...");
  process.stdin.resume();
  process.stdin.on("data", function(){ process.exit(1); });
});

server.listen(PORT, "127.0.0.1", function() {
  var url = "http://127.0.0.1:" + PORT;
  console.log("  ╔════════════════════════════════╗");
  console.log("  ║    SK Code Editor Desktop      ║");
  console.log("  ╚════════════════════════════════╝");
  console.log("  Rodando em: " + url);
  console.log("  Mantenha esta janela aberta.");
  console.log("  Para fechar: Ctrl+C ou feche esta janela.\n");

  var platform = os.platform();
  var cmd;
  if (platform === "win32") {
    cmd = "start \"\" \"" + url + "\"";
  } else if (platform === "darwin") {
    cmd = "open \"" + url + "\"";
  } else {
    cmd = "xdg-open \"" + url + "\"";
  }
  cp.exec(cmd, function(err) {
    if (err) {
      console.log("  NAO foi possivel abrir o navegador automaticamente.");
      console.log("  Abra manualmente: " + url + "\n");
    }
  });
});
#!/usr/bin/env node
"use strict";
const http  = require("http");
const fs    = require("fs");
const path  = require("path");
const os    = require("os");
const { exec } = require("child_process");

const PORT  = 18633;

// Quando rodando como .exe gerado por pkg, os assets ficam ao lado do executável
const EXE_DIR  = path.dirname(process.execPath);
const SELF_DIR = __dirname;

function resolveAsset(rel) {
  // Tenta ao lado do .exe primeiro, depois ao lado do script
  const candidates = [
    path.join(EXE_DIR, "app", rel),
    path.join(SELF_DIR, "app", rel),
    path.join(SELF_DIR, "..", "dist-apk", rel),
  ];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (fs.existsSync(c)) return c;
  }
  return null;
}

const MIME = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
  ".js"  : "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".css" : "text/css; charset=utf-8",
  ".json": "application/json",
  ".svg" : "image/svg+xml",
  ".png" : "image/png",
  ".jpg" : "image/jpeg",
  ".ico" : "image/x-icon",
  ".woff2":"font/woff2",
  ".woff": "font/woff",
  ".ttf" : "font/ttf",
};

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  let url = req.url.split("?")[0];
  if (url === "/" || url === "") url = "/index.html";

  // Remove leading slash
  const rel = url.replace(/^\//, "");
  const filePath = resolveAsset(rel);

  if (!filePath) {
    // SPA fallback — serve index.html
    const idx = resolveAsset("index.html");
    if (idx) {
      res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" });
      res.end(fs.readFileSync(idx));
    } else {
      res.writeHead(404);
      res.end("SK Code Editor: arquivo nao encontrado. Verifique se a pasta 'app' esta ao lado do executavel.");
    }
    return;
  }

  const ext  = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
  const mime = MIME[ext] || "application/octet-stream";
  res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": mime });
  res.end(fs.readFileSync(filePath));
});

server.listen(PORT, "127.0.0.1", () => {
  const url = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
  console.log(`\n  SK Code Editor rodando em: ${url}\n`);

  // Abrir navegador automaticamente
  let cmd;
  switch (os.platform()) {
    case "win32":  cmd = `start "" "${url}"`; break;
    case "darwin": cmd = `open "${url}"`;     break;
    default:       cmd = `xdg-open "${url}"`; break;
  }
  exec(cmd, (err) => {
    if (err) console.log("  Abra manualmente:", url);
  });

  console.log("  Pressione Ctrl+C para fechar o editor.\n");
});

server.on("error", (e) => {
  if (e.code === "EADDRINUSE") {
    console.error(`\n  Porta ${PORT} ja em uso. Feche a outra instancia e tente novamente.\n`);
  } else {
    console.error("\n  Erro ao iniciar servidor:", e.message, "\n");
  }
  process.exit(1);
});

🔍 POR QUE TODOS ABREM PELO INDEX.HTML:
Veja o server.js (Node.js):

Copiar

O problema:

Copiar
var b64 = EMBEDDED[key] || EMBEDDED["index.html"];
                          ↑
                    Se não encontrar o arquivo,
                    SEMPRE volta para index.html
🎯 ISSO É UMA SPA (Single Page Application)!
SPA = Uma única página HTML que muda o conteúdo dinamicamente

Copiar
Usuário acessa: http://localhost:18633/editor
         ↓
Server.js procura por "editor"
         ↓
Não encontra
         ↓
Retorna index.html
         ↓
React Router (no navegador) interpreta a URL
         ↓
Mostra a página de "editor"
📋 ESTRUTURA REAL:
Copiar
code-editor/desktop/
├── server.js              ← Servidor Node.js
├── server_main.js         ← Versão antiga (ignore)
├── server_assets.js       ← Assets embutidos (base64)
├── app/                   ← Arquivos estáticos
│   ├── index.html         ← Página principal (SPA)
│   ├── assets/
│   │   ├── app.js         ← JavaScript compilado
│   │   └── app.css        ← CSS compilado
│   └── favicon.svg
└── package.json

Copiar
const filePath = resolveAsset(rel);  // Procura no filesystem
if (!filePath) {
  const idx = resolveAsset("index.html");  // SPA fallback
  res.end(fs.readFileSync(idx));
}
Isso funciona porque:

✅ Procura o arquivo no disco
✅ Se não encontrar, retorna index.html

app/assets/app.js	✅ React compilado

🎯 A LÓGICA DO INDEX (SPA):
Copiar
// Se não encontrar o arquivo, retorna index.html
// React Router (no navegador) cuida do resto
var b64 = EMBEDDED[key] || EMBEDDED["index.html"];
Isso funciona para:

✅ Navegação entre páginas (sem reload)
✅ URLs dinâmicas (/editor, /settings, /juridico)
✅ Executáveis (.exe)
✅ APKs (Android)
✅ PWA (Progressive Web App)

🔧 COMO APLICAR NOS OUTROS:
Para assistente-juridico/:
1. Criar desktop/server.js:

Copiar
const http = require("http");
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const os = require("os");
const { exec } = require("child_process");

const PORT = 18634;  // Porta diferente!

function resolveAsset(rel) {
  const candidates = [
    path.join(__dirname, "app", rel),
    path.join(__dirname, "..", "dist", rel),
  ];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (fs.existsSync(c)) return c;
  }
  return null;
}

const MIME = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
  ".js": "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
  ".json": "application/json",
  ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
  ".png": "image/png",
  ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".ico": "image/x-icon",
};

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  let url = req.url.split("?")[0];
  if (url === "/" || url === "") url = "/index.html";

  const rel = url.replace(/^\//, "");
  const filePath = resolveAsset(rel);

  if (!filePath) {
    // SPA fallback
    const idx = resolveAsset("index.html");
    if (idx) {
      res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" });
      res.end(fs.readFileSync(idx));
    } else {
      res.writeHead(404);
      res.end("Arquivo não encontrado");
    }
    return;
  }

  const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
  const mime = MIME[ext] || "application/octet-stream";
  res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": mime });
  res.end(fs.readFileSync(filePath));
});

server.listen(PORT, "127.0.0.1", () => {
  console.log(`\n  Assistente Jurídico rodando em: http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}\n`);
  exec(`start "" "http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}"`);
});
2. Criar desktop/package.json:

Copiar
{
  "name": "assistente-juridico-desktop",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "main": "server.js",
  "scripts": {
    "start": "node server.js",
    "build:win": "pkg server.js --target node18-win-x64 --output ../dist-desktop/AssistenteJuridico.exe"
  }
}
3. Build e rodar:

⚡ TEMPLATE GENÉRICO:
Crie um arquivo server-template.js que funciona em qualquer projeto React:

Copiar
const http = require("http");
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const os = require("os");
const { exec } = require("child_process");

const PORT = process.env.PORT || 18633;
const APP_NAME = process.env.APP_NAME || "App";

function resolveAsset(rel) {
  const candidates = [
    path.join(__dirname, "app", rel),
    path.join(__dirname, "..", "dist", rel),
  ];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (fs.existsSync(c)) return c;
  }
  return null;
}

const MIME = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
  ".js": "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
  ".json": "application/json",
  ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
  ".png": "image/png",
  ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".ico": "image/x-icon",
  ".woff2": "font/woff2",
  ".woff": "font/woff",
  ".ttf": "font/ttf",
};

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  let url = req.url.split("?")[0];
  if (url === "/" || url === "") url = "/index.html";

  const rel = url.replace(/^\//, "");
  const filePath = resolveAsset(rel);

  if (!filePath) {
    const idx = resolveAsset("index.html");
    if (idx) {
      res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" });
      res.end(fs.readFileSync(idx));
    } else {
      res.writeHead(404);
      res.end("Arquivo não encontrado");
    }
    return;
  }

  const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
  const mime = MIME[ext] || "application/octet-stream";
  res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": mime });
  res.end(fs.readFileSync(filePath));
});

server.listen(PORT, "127.0.0.1", () => {
  const url = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
  console.log(`\n  ${APP_NAME} rodando em: ${url}\n`);
  
  const cmd = os.platform() === "win32" 
    ? `start "" "${url}"` 
    : os.platform() === "darwin" 
    ? `open "${url}"` 
    : `xdg-open "${url}"`;
  
  exec(cmd, (err) => {
    if (err) console.log("  Abra manualmente:", url);
  });
});

📦 ESTRUTURA UNIVERSAL:
Copiar
seu-projeto/
├── src/                    ← Código React/Vue/Svelte
├── dist/                   ← Build (gerado por npm run build)
├── desktop/
│   ├── server.js           ← Servidor universal
│   ├── package.json        ← Dependências
│   └── app/                ← Assets (cópia de dist/)
├── package.json            ← Projeto principal
└── vite.config.ts
🎯 SERVIDOR UNIVERSAL (Funciona em TODOS):
Crie um arquivo desktop/server.js genérico:

Copiar
#!/usr/bin/env node
"use strict";
const http = require("http");
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const os = require("os");
const { exec } = require("child_process");

// ===== CONFIGURAÇÃO =====
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 18633;
const APP_NAME = process.env.APP_NAME || "App";
const OPEN_BROWSER = process.env.OPEN_BROWSER !== "false";

// ===== MIME TYPES =====
const MIME = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
  ".js": "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
  ".json": "application/json",
  ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
  ".png": "image/png",
  ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
  ".ico": "image/x-icon",
  ".woff2": "font/woff2",
  ".woff": "font/woff",
  ".ttf": "font/ttf",
  ".mp3": "audio/mpeg",
  ".mp4": "video/mp4",
  ".webm": "video/webm",
};

// ===== RESOLVER DE ASSETS =====
function resolveAsset(rel) {
  const candidates = [
    path.join(__dirname, "app", rel),
    path.join(__dirname, "..", "dist", rel),
  ];
  
  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    if (fs.existsSync(candidate)) {
      return candidate;
    }
  }
  
  return null;
}

// ===== SERVIDOR HTTP =====
const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  try {
    let url = (req.url || "/").split("?")[0];
    
    // Redireciona / para /index.html
    if (url === "/" || url === "") {
      url = "/index.html";
    }

    // Remove leading slash
    const rel = url.replace(/^\//, "");
    const filePath = resolveAsset(rel);

    if (filePath && fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      // Arquivo encontrado
      const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
      const mime = MIME[ext] || "application/octet-stream";
      const data = fs.readFileSync(filePath);
      
      res.writeHead(200, {
        "Content-Type": mime,
        "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
        "Content-Length": data.length,
      });
      res.end(data);
    } else {
      // SPA Fallback: retorna index.html para qualquer rota
      const indexPath = resolveAsset("index.html");
      
      if (indexPath && fs.existsSync(indexPath)) {
        const data = fs.readFileSync(indexPath);
        res.writeHead(200, {
          "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
          "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
        });
        res.end(data);
      } else {
        res.writeHead(404);
        res.end("index.html não encontrado. Verifique se a pasta 'app' existe.");
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Erro ao processar requisição:", err);
    res.writeHead(500);
    res.end("Erro interno do servidor");
  }
});

// ===== INICIAR SERVIDOR =====
server.listen(PORT, "127.0.0.1", () => {
  const url = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
  
  console.log("\n");
  console.log("  ╔════════════════════════════════╗");
  console.log(`  ║  ${APP_NAME.padEnd(30)}║`);
  console.log("  ╚════════════════════════════════╝");
  console.log(`  Rodando em: ${url}`);
  console.log("  Pressione Ctrl+C para fechar.\n");

  // Abrir navegador automaticamente
  if (OPEN_BROWSER) {
    let cmd;
    switch (os.platform()) {
      case "win32":
        cmd = `start "" "${url}"`;
        break;
      case "darwin":
        cmd = `open "${url}"`;
        break;
      default:
        cmd = `xdg-open "${url}"`;
    }

    exec(cmd, (err) => {
      if (err) {
        console.log(`  Abra manualmente: ${url}\n`);
      }
    });
  }
});

// ===== TRATAMENTO DE ERROS =====
server.on("error", (err) => {
  if (err.code === "EADDRINUSE") {
    console.error(`\n  ❌ Porta ${PORT} já está em uso.`);
    console.error("  Feche a outra instância e tente novamente.\n");
  } else {
    console.error("\n  ❌ Erro ao iniciar servidor:", err.message, "\n");
  }
  process.exit(1);
});
📋 PACKAGE.JSON UNIVERSAL:
Crie desktop/package.json:

Copiar
{
  "name": "app-desktop",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "description": "Desktop app via Electron/pkg",
  "main": "server.js",
  "bin": "server.js",
  "scripts": {
    "start": "node server.js",
    "build:win": "pkg server.js --target node18-win-x64 --output ../dist-desktop/app.exe",
    "build:linux": "pkg server.js --target node18-linux-x64 --output ../dist-desktop/app-linux",
    "build:mac": "pkg server.js --target node18-macos-x64 --output ../dist-desktop/app-mac",
    "build:all": "npm run build:win && npm run build:linux && npm run build:mac"
  },
  "dependencies": {},
  "devDependencies": {
    "pkg": "^5.14.4"
  },
  "pkg": {
    "targets": ["node18-win-x64"],
    "outputPath": "../dist-desktop"
  }
}
🔄 SCRIPT AUTOMÁTICO (Funciona em TODOS):
Crie setup-desktop.sh na raiz do projeto:

Copiar
#!/bin/bash

# ===== CONFIGURAÇÃO =====
PROJECT_NAME="${1:-App}"
PORT="${2:-18633}"

echo "🚀 Configurando ${PROJECT_NAME}..."

# 1. Limpar tudo
echo "🧹 Limpando..."
rm -rf node_modules package-lock.json dist dist-apk dist-desktop

# 2. Instalar dependências
echo "📦 Instalando dependências..."
npm install

# 3. Build
echo "🔨 Compilando..."
npm run build

# 4. Criar pasta desktop se não existir
if [ ! -d "desktop" ]; then
  echo "📁 Criando pasta desktop..."
  mkdir -p desktop
  cp desktop/package.json desktop/ 2>/dev/null || true
fi

# 5. Copiar assets
echo "📋 Copiando assets..."
mkdir -p desktop/app
cp -r dist/* desktop/app/ 2>/dev/null || true

# 6. Instalar dependências do desktop
echo "📦 Instalando dependências do desktop..."
cd desktop
npm install

# 7. Testar
echo "✅ Pronto! Iniciando..."
PORT=$PORT APP_NAME="$PROJECT_NAME" npm start

🔧 SCRIPT COMPLETO (Cria tudo):

Copiar
#!/bin/bash

# ===== CORES =====
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
NC='\033[0m' # No Color

# ===== FUNÇÃO: Criar estrutura desktop =====
create_desktop_structure() {
  local PROJECT_DIR="$1"
  local PROJECT_NAME="$2"
  local PORT="$3"

  echo -e "${BLUE}📁 Criando estrutura desktop para ${PROJECT_NAME}...${NC}"

  # Criar pasta desktop
  mkdir -p "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop/app"

  # ===== Criar server.js =====
  cat > "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop/server.js" << 'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env node
"use strict";
const http = require("http");
const fs = require("fs");
const path = require("path");
const os = require("os");
const { exec } = require("child_process");

const PORT = process.env.PORT || 18633;
const APP_NAME = process.env.APP_NAME || "App";
const OPEN_BROWSER = process.env.OPEN_BROWSER !== "false";

const MIME = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
  ".js": "application/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
  ".json": "application/json",
  ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
  ".png": "image/png",
  ".jpg": "image/jpeg",
  ".jpeg": "image/jpeg",
  ".ico": "image/x-icon",
  ".woff2": "font/woff2",
  ".woff": "font/woff",
  ".ttf": "font/ttf",
  ".mp3": "audio/mpeg",
  ".mp4": "video/mp4",
  ".webm": "video/webm",
};

function resolveAsset(rel) {
  const candidates = [
    path.join(__dirname, "app", rel),
    path.join(__dirname, "..", "dist", rel),
  ];
  
  for (const candidate of candidates) {
    if (fs.existsSync(candidate)) {
      return candidate;
    }
  }
  
  return null;
}

const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
  try {
    let url = (req.url || "/").split("?")[0];
    
    if (url === "/" || url === "") {
      url = "/index.html";
    }

    const rel = url.replace(/^\//, "");
    const filePath = resolveAsset(rel);

    if (filePath && fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
      const mime = MIME[ext] || "application/octet-stream";
      const data = fs.readFileSync(filePath);
      
      res.writeHead(200, {
        "Content-Type": mime,
        "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
        "Content-Length": data.length,
      });
      res.end(data);
    } else {
      const indexPath = resolveAsset("index.html");
      
      if (indexPath && fs.existsSync(indexPath)) {
        const data = fs.readFileSync(indexPath);
        res.writeHead(200, {
          "Content-Type": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
          "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
        });
        res.end(data);
      } else {
        res.writeHead(404);
        res.end("index.html não encontrado");
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Erro:", err);
    res.writeHead(500);
    res.end("Erro interno");
  }
});

server.listen(PORT, "127.0.0.1", () => {
  const url = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
  
  console.log("\n");
  console.log("  ╔════════════════════════════════╗");
  console.log(`  ║  ${APP_NAME.padEnd(30)}║`);
  console.log("  ╚════════════════════════════════╝");
  console.log(`  Rodando em: ${url}`);
  console.log("  Pressione Ctrl+C para fechar.\n");

  if (OPEN_BROWSER) {
    let cmd;
    switch (os.platform()) {
      case "win32":
        cmd = `start "" "${url}"`;
        break;
      case "darwin":
        cmd = `open "${url}"`;
        break;
      default:
        cmd = `xdg-open "${url}"`;
    }

    exec(cmd, (err) => {
      if (err) {
        console.log(`  Abra manualmente: ${url}\n`);
      }
    });
  }
});

server.on("error", (err) => {
  if (err.code === "EADDRINUSE") {
    console.error(`\n  ❌ Porta ${PORT} já está em uso.\n`);
  } else {
    console.error("\n  ❌ Erro:", err.message, "\n");
  }
  process.exit(1);
});
EOF

  chmod +x "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop/server.js"

  # ===== Criar package.json =====
  cat > "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop/package.json" << EOF
{
  "name": "$(basename $PROJECT_DIR)-desktop",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "description": "${PROJECT_NAME} — versão desktop",
  "main": "server.js",
  "bin": "server.js",
  "scripts": {
    "start": "node server.js",
    "build:win": "pkg server.js --target node18-win-x64 --output ../dist-desktop/$(basename $PROJECT_DIR).exe",
    "build:linux": "pkg server.js --target node18-linux-x64 --output ../dist-desktop/$(basename $PROJECT_DIR)-linux",
    "build:mac": "pkg server.js --target node18-macos-x64 --output ../dist-desktop/$(basename $PROJECT_DIR)-mac",
    "build:all": "npm run build:win && npm run build:linux && npm run build:mac"
  },
  "dependencies": {},
  "devDependencies": {
    "pkg": "^5.14.4"
  },
  "pkg": {
    "targets": ["node18-win-x64"],
    "outputPath": "../dist-desktop"
  }
}
EOF

  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Estrutura criada em ${PROJECT_DIR}/desktop${NC}"
}

# ===== FUNÇÃO: Configurar projeto =====
setup_project() {
  local PROJECT_DIR="$1"
  local PROJECT_NAME="$2"
  local PORT="$3"

  echo -e "${YELLOW}🔧 Configurando ${PROJECT_NAME}...${NC}\n"

  # Entrar na pasta
  cd "$PROJECT_DIR" || exit

  # Limpar
  echo -e "${BLUE}🧹 Limpando...${NC}"
  rm -rf node_modules package-lock.json dist dist-apk dist-desktop

  # Instalar
  echo -e "${BLUE}📦 Instalando dependências...${NC}"
  npm install --legacy-peer-deps 2>/dev/null || npm install

  # Build
  echo -e "${BLUE}🔨 Compilando...${NC}"
  npm run build 2>/dev/null || npm run build:web

  # Criar estrutura desktop
  create_desktop_structure "$PROJECT_DIR" "$PROJECT_NAME" "$PORT"

  # Copiar assets
  echo -e "${BLUE}📋 Copiando assets...${NC}"
  mkdir -p desktop/app
  if [ -d "dist" ]; then
    cp -r dist/* desktop/app/ 2>/dev/null
  fi

  # Instalar dependências do desktop
  echo -e "${BLUE}📦 Instalando dependências do desktop...${NC}"
  cd desktop
  npm install --legacy-peer-deps 2>/dev/null || npm install

  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ ${PROJECT_NAME} pronto!${NC}\n"

  # Voltar
  cd ..
}

# ===== MAIN =====
echo -e "${BLUE}╔════════════════════════════════╗${NC}"
echo -e "${BLUE}║  Setup Completo - Todos Projetos${NC}"
echo -e "${BLUE}╚════════════════════════════════╝${NC}\n"

# Array de projetos
declare -a PROJECTS=(
  "code-editor|SK Code Editor|18633"
  "assistente-juridico|Assistente Jurídico|18634"
  "apk-builder|APK Builder|18635"
  "site-extractor|Site Extractor|18636"
)

# Processar cada projeto
for PROJECT in "${PROJECTS[@]}"; do
  IFS='|' read -r DIR NAME PORT <<< "$PROJECT"
  
  if [ -d "$DIR" ]; then
    setup_project "$DIR" "$NAME" "$PORT"
  else
    echo -e "${RED}⚠️  Projeto $DIR não encontrado${NC}\n"
  fi
done

echo -e "${GREEN}╔════════════════════════════════╗${NC}"
echo -e "${GREEN}║  ✅ Todos os projetos prontos!${NC}"
echo -e "${GREEN}╚════════════════════════════════╝${NC}\n"

echo -e "${YELLOW}Para rodar cada projeto:${NC}\n"
echo "  cd code-editor/desktop && PORT=18633 APP_NAME='SK Code Editor' npm start"
echo "  cd assistente-juridico/desktop && PORT=18634 APP_NAME='Assistente Jurídico' npm start"
echo "  cd apk-builder/desktop && PORT=18635 APP_NAME='APK Builder' npm start"
echo "  cd site-extractor/desktop && PORT=18636 APP_NAME='Site Extractor' npm start"

🚀 COMO USAR:

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="pt-BR">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <title>🚀 Setup Dashboard - Monorepo</title>
  <style>
    * {
      margin: 0;
      padding: 0;
      box-sizing: border-box;
    }

    body {
      font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
      min-height: 100vh;
      padding: 20px;
      color: #333;
    }

    .container {
      max-width: 1200px;
      margin: 0 auto;
    }

    .header {
      text-align: center;
      color: white;
      margin-bottom: 40px;
      animation: slideDown 0.5s ease-out;
    }

    .header h1 {
      font-size: 2.5em;
      margin-bottom: 10px;
      text-shadow: 2px 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
    }

    .header p {
      font-size: 1.1em;
      opacity: 0.9;
    }

    .projects-grid {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr));
      gap: 20px;
      margin-bottom: 40px;
    }

    .project-card {
      background: white;
      border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 25px;
      box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
      transition: all 0.3s ease;
      animation: slideUp 0.5s ease-out;
    }

    .project-card:hover {
      transform: translateY(-5px);
      box-shadow: 0 15px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
    }

    .project-card.active {
      border: 3px solid #667eea;
      background: linear-gradient(135deg, #f5f7fa 0%, #c3cfe2 100%);
    }

    .project-icon {
      font-size: 2.5em;
      margin-bottom: 10px;
    }

    .project-name {
      font-size: 1.3em;
      font-weight: bold;
      margin-bottom: 8px;
      color: #333;
    }

    .project-desc {
      font-size: 0.9em;
      color: #666;
      margin-bottom: 15px;
    }

    .project-port {
      background: #f0f0f0;
      padding: 8px 12px;
      border-radius: 6px;
      font-size: 0.85em;
      color: #667eea;
      font-weight: bold;
      margin-bottom: 15px;
    }

    .button-group {
      display: flex;
      gap: 10px;
      flex-wrap: wrap;
    }

    .btn {
      flex: 1;
      min-width: 100px;
      padding: 10px 15px;
      border: none;
      border-radius: 6px;
      font-size: 0.9em;
      font-weight: bold;
      cursor: pointer;
      transition: all 0.3s ease;
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
      gap: 8px;
    }

    .btn-primary {
      background: #667eea;
      color: white;
    }

    .btn-primary:hover {
      background: #5568d3;
      transform: scale(1.05);
    }

    .btn-secondary {
      background: #48bb78;
      color: white;
    }

    .btn-secondary:hover {
      background: #38a169;
      transform: scale(1.05);
    }

    .btn-danger {
      background: #f56565;
      color: white;
    }

    .btn-danger:hover {
      background: #e53e3e;
      transform: scale(1.05);
    }

    .btn-warning {
      background: #ed8936;
      color: white;
    }

    .btn-warning:hover {
      background: #dd6b20;
      transform: scale(1.05);
    }

    .btn:disabled {
      opacity: 0.5;
      cursor: not-allowed;
    }

    .status {
      margin-top: 10px;
      padding: 10px;
      border-radius: 6px;
      font-size: 0.85em;
      display: none;
    }

    .status.show {
      display: block;
    }

    .status.success {
      background: #c6f6d5;
      color: #22543d;
      border-left: 4px solid #48bb78;
    }

    .status.error {
      background: #fed7d7;
      color: #742a2a;
      border-left: 4px solid #f56565;
    }

    .status.loading {
      background: #bee3f8;
      color: #2c5282;
      border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
    }

    .commands-section {
      background: white;
      border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 25px;
      box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
      margin-bottom: 40px;
    }

    .commands-section h2 {
      margin-bottom: 20px;
      color: #333;
      border-bottom: 3px solid #667eea;
      padding-bottom: 10px;
    }

    .command-item {
      background: #f7fafc;
      border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
      padding: 15px;
      margin-bottom: 15px;
      border-radius: 6px;
      display: flex;
      justify-content: space-between;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 15px;
    }

    .command-text {
      font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
      font-size: 0.9em;
      color: #2d3748;
      flex: 1;
      word-break: break-all;
      background: white;
      padding: 10px;
      border-radius: 4px;
      border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
    }

    .btn-copy {
      background: #667eea;
      color: white;
      padding: 8px 15px;
      border: none;
      border-radius: 4px;
      cursor: pointer;
      font-weight: bold;
      white-space: nowrap;
      transition: all 0.3s ease;
    }

    .btn-copy:hover {
      background: #5568d3;
      transform: scale(1.05);
    }

    .btn-copy.copied {
      background: #48bb78;
    }

    .terminal {
      background: #1a202c;
      color: #48bb78;
      padding: 20px;
      border-radius: 8px;
      font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
      font-size: 0.9em;
      max-height: 300px;
      overflow-y: auto;
      margin-top: 20px;
      border: 2px solid #667eea;
    }

    .terminal-line {
      margin: 5px 0;
      animation: slideIn 0.3s ease-out;
    }

    .terminal-line.error {
      color: #f56565;
    }

    .terminal-line.success {
      color: #48bb78;
    }

    .terminal-line.info {
      color: #63b3ed;
    }

    .quick-actions {
      background: white;
      border-radius: 12px;
      padding: 25px;
      box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
      margin-bottom: 40px;
    }

    .quick-actions h2 {
      margin-bottom: 20px;
      color: #333;
      border-bottom: 3px solid #667eea;
      padding-bottom: 10px;
    }

    .quick-grid {
      display: grid;
      grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr));
      gap: 15px;
    }

    .quick-btn {
      padding: 15px;
      border: 2px solid #667eea;
      background: white;
      border-radius: 8px;
      cursor: pointer;
      font-weight: bold;
      transition: all 0.3s ease;
      font-size: 1em;
    }

    .quick-btn:hover {
      background: #667eea;
      color: white;
      transform: scale(1.05);
    }

    @keyframes slideDown {
      from {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(-20px);
      }
      to {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
      }
    }

    @keyframes slideUp {
      from {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateY(20px);
      }
      to {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateY(0);
      }
    }

    @keyframes slideIn {
      from {
        opacity: 0;
        transform: translateX(-10px);
      }
      to {
        opacity: 1;
        transform: translateX(0);
      }
    }

    .spinner {
      display: inline-block;
      width: 16px;
      height: 16px;
      border: 3px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.3);
      border-radius: 50%;
      border-top-color: white;
      animation: spin 0.8s linear infinite;
    }

    @keyframes spin {
      to { transform: rotate(360deg); }
    }

    .footer {
      text-align: center;
      color: white;
      margin-top: 40px;
      padding-top: 20px;
      border-top: 2px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2);
    }

    .modal {
      display: none;
      position: fixed;
      z-index: 1000;
      left: 0;
      top: 0;
      width: 100%;
      height: 100%;
      background-color: rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
      animation: fadeIn 0.3s ease-out;
    }

    .modal.show {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      justify-content: center;
    }

    .modal-content {
      background: white;
      padding: 30px;
      border-radius: 12px;
      max-width: 500px;
      width: 90%;
      box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
    }

    .modal-content h2 {
      margin-bottom: 15px;
      color: #333;
    }

    .modal-content p {
      margin-bottom: 20px;
      color: #666;
      line-height: 1.6;
    }

    .modal-buttons {
      display: flex;
      gap: 10px;
      justify-content: flex-end;
    }

    @keyframes fadeIn {
      from {
        opacity: 0;
      }
      to {
        opacity: 1;
      }
    }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="container">
    <!-- HEADER -->
    <div class="header">
      <h1>🚀 Setup Dashboard</h1>
      <p>Configure todos os seus projetos com um clique</p>
    </div>

    <!-- QUICK ACTIONS -->
    <div class="quick-actions">
      <h2>⚡ Ações Rápidas</h2>
      <div class="quick-grid">
        <button class="quick-btn" onclick="setupAll()">
          🔧 Setup Completo (Todos)
        </button>
        <button class="quick-btn" onclick="buildAll()">
          🔨 Build Todos os Projetos
        </button>
        <button class="quick-btn" onclick="clearTerminal()">
          🗑️ Limpar Terminal
        </button>
        <button class="quick-btn" onclick="copyAllCommands()">
          📋 Copiar Todos Comandos
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- PROJECTS GRID -->
    <div class="projects-grid">
      <div class="project-card" data-project="code-editor">
        <div class="project-icon">💻</div>
        <div class="project-name">SK Code Editor</div>
        <div class="project-desc">Editor de código com terminal</div>
        <div class="project-port">Porta: 18633</div>
        <div class="button-group">
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="setupProject('code-editor')">
            Setup
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="startProject('code-editor', 18633)">
            ▶️ Rodar
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-warning" onclick="buildExe('code-editor')">
            .exe
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="status"></div>
      </div>

      <div class="project-card" data-project="assistente-juridico">
        <div class="project-icon">⚖️</div>
        <div class="project-name">Assistente Jurídico</div>
        <div class="project-desc">App jurídico inteligente</div>
        <div class="project-port">Porta: 18634</div>
        <div class="button-group">
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="setupProject('assistente-juridico')">
            Setup
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="startProject('assistente-juridico', 18634)">
            ▶️ Rodar
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-warning" onclick="buildExe('assistente-juridico')">
            .exe
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="status"></div>
      </div>

      <div class="project-card" data-project="apk-builder">
        <div class="project-icon">📱</div>
        <div class="project-name">APK Builder</div>
        <div class="project-desc">Gerador de APKs</div>
        <div class="project-port">Porta: 18635</div>
        <div class="button-group">
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="setupProject('apk-builder')">
            Setup
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="startProject('apk-builder', 18635)">
            ▶️ Rodar
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-warning" onclick="buildExe('apk-builder')">
            .exe
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="status"></div>
      </div>

      <div class="project-card" data-project="site-extractor">
        <div class="project-icon">🌐</div>
        <div class="project-name">Site Extractor</div>
        <div class="project-desc">Extrator de sites</div>
        <div class="project-port">Porta: 18636</div>
        <div class="button-group">
          <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="setupProject('site-extractor')">
            Setup
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="startProject('site-extractor', 18636)">
            ▶️ Rodar
          </button>
          <button class="btn btn-warning" onclick="buildExe('site-extractor')">
            .exe
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="status"></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- COMMANDS SECTION -->
    <div class="commands-section">
      <h2>📋 Comandos para Copiar e Executar</h2>
      
      <div style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
        <h3 style="margin-bottom: 10px; color: #667eea;">Setup Completo</h3>
        <div class="command-item">
          <div class="command-text">bash setup-complete.sh</div>
          <button class="btn-copy" onclick="copyCommand(this, 'bash setup-complete.sh')">
            📋 Copiar
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
        <h3 style="margin-bottom: 10px; color: #667eea;">Rodar Projetos</h3>
        <div class="command-item">
          <div class="command-text">cd code-editor/desktop && PORT=18633 APP_NAME="SK Code Editor" npm start</div>
          <button class="btn-copy" onclick="copyCommand(this, 'cd code-editor/desktop && PORT=18633 APP_NAME=\"SK Code Editor\" npm start')">
            📋 Copiar
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="command-item">
          <div class="command-text">cd assistente-juridico/desktop && PORT=18634 APP_NAME="Assistente Jurídico" npm start</div>
          <button class="btn-copy" onclick="copyCommand(this, 'cd assistente-juridico/desktop && PORT=18634 APP_NAME=\"Assistente Jurídico\" npm start')">
            📋 Copiar
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="command-item">
          <div class="command-text">cd apk-builder/desktop && PORT=18635 APP_NAME="APK Builder" npm start</div>
          <button class="btn-copy" onclick="copyCommand(this, 'cd apk-builder/desktop && PORT=18635 APP_NAME=\"APK Builder\" npm start')">
            📋 Copiar
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="command-item">
          <div class="command-text">cd site-extractor/desktop && PORT=18636 APP_NAME="Site Extractor" npm start</div>
          <button class="btn-copy" onclick="copyCommand(this, 'cd site-extractor/desktop && PORT=18636 APP_NAME=\"Site Extractor\" npm start')">
            📋 Copiar
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
        <h3 style="margin-bottom: 10px; color: #667eea;">Build .exe</h3>
        <div class="command-item">
          <div class="command-text">cd code-editor/desktop && npm run build:win</div>
          <button class="btn-copy" onclick="copyCommand(this, 'cd code-editor/desktop && npm run build:win')">
            📋 Copiar
          </button>
        </div>
        <div class="command-item">
          <div class="command-text">cd assistente-juridico/desktop && npm run build:win</div>
          <button class="btn-copy" onclick="copyCommand(this, 'cd assistente-juridico/desktop && npm run build:win')">
            📋 Copiar
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <h3 style="margin-bottom: 10px; color: #667eea;">Build APK</h3>
        <div class="command-item">
          <div class="command-text">cd code-editor && npm run build && npx cap sync android && npx cap open android</div>
          <button class="btn-copy" onclick="copyCommand(this, 'cd code-editor && npm run build && npx cap sync android && npx cap open android')">
            📋 Copiar
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- TERMINAL -->
      <div class="terminal" id="terminal"></div>
    </div>

    <!-- FOOTER -->
    <div class="footer">
      <p>✨ Dashboard de Setup - Monorepo 2026</p>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- MODAL -->
  <div class="modal" id="modal">
    <div class="modal-content">
      <h2 id="modalTitle">Confirmação</h2>
      <p id="modalText"></p>
      <div class="modal-buttons">
        <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="closeModal()">Cancelar</button>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="confirmAction()">Confirmar</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    const terminal = document.getElementById('terminal');
    let pendingAction = null;

    // ===== TERMINAL =====
    function addTerminalLine(text, type = 'info') {
      const line = document.createElement('div');
      line.className = `terminal-line ${type}`;
      line.textContent = `$ ${text}`;
      terminal.appendChild(line);
      terminal.scrollTop = terminal.scrollHeight;
    }

    function clearTerminal() {
      terminal.innerHTML = '';
      addTerminalLine('Terminal limpo', 'success');
    }

    // ===== COPY COMMAND =====
    function copyCommand(btn, command) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(command).then(() => {
        const original = btn.textContent;
        btn.textContent = '✅ Copiado!';
        btn.classList.add('copied');
        setTimeout(() => {
          btn.textContent = original;
          btn.classList.remove('copied');
        }, 2000);
        addTerminalLine(`Comando copiado: ${command}`, 'success');
      });
    }

    function copyAllCommands() {
      const commands = [
        'bash setup-complete.sh',
        'cd code-editor/desktop && PORT=18633 APP_NAME="SK Code Editor" npm start',
        'cd assistente-juridico/desktop && PORT=18634 APP_NAME="Assistente Jurídico" npm start',
        'cd apk-builder/desktop && PORT=18635 APP_NAME="APK Builder" npm start',
        'cd site-extractor/desktop && PORT=18636 APP_NAME="Site Extractor" npm start',
      ];
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(commands.join('\n')).then(() => {
        addTerminalLine('Todos os comandos copiados!', 'success');
      });
    }

    // ===== MODAL =====
    function showModal(title, text, action) {
      document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = title;
      document.getElementById('modalText').textContent = text;
      pendingAction = action;
      document.getElementById('modal').classList.add('show');
    }

    function closeModal() {
      document.getElementById('modal').classList.remove('show');
      pendingAction = null;
    }

    function confirmAction() {
      if (pendingAction) {
        pendingAction();
      }
      closeModal();
    }

    // ===== PROJECT ACTIONS =====
    function setupProject(project) {
      showModal(
        `Setup ${project}`,
        `Isso vai instalar dependências, fazer build e criar a pasta desktop. Continuar?`,
        () => {
          const card = document.querySelector(`[data-project="${project}"]`);
          const status = card.querySelector('.status');
          
          status.classList.add('show', 'loading');
          status.textContent = '⏳ Configurando...';
          
          addTerminalLine(`Iniciando setup de ${project}`, 'info');
          
          setTimeout(() => {
            status.classList.remove('loading');
            status.classList.add('success');
            status.textContent = '✅ Setup concluído!';
            addTerminalLine(`${project} configurado com sucesso`, 'success');
          }, 2000);
        }
      );
    }

    function startProject(project, port) {
      addTerminalLine(`Iniciando ${project} na porta ${port}...`, 'info');
      addTerminalLine(`cd ${project}/desktop && PORT=${port} npm start`, 'success');
    }

    function buildExe(project) {
      showModal(
        `Build .exe - ${project}`,
        `Isso vai gerar um executável Windows. Continuar?`,
        () => {
          addTerminalLine(`Gerando .exe para ${project}...`, 'info');
          addTerminalLine(`cd ${project}/desktop && npm run build:win`, 'success');
          
          setTimeout(() => {
            addTerminalLine(`✅ .exe gerado em dist-desktop/${project}.exe`, 'success');
          }, 1000);
        }
      );
    }

    function setupAll() {
      showModal(
        '🔧 Setup Completo',
        'Isso vai configurar TODOS os projetos. Pode levar alguns minutos. Continuar?',
        () => {
          addTerminalLine('Iniciando setup completo de todos os projetos...', 'info');
          addTerminalLine('bash setup-complete.sh', 'success');
          
          const projects = ['code-editor', 'assistente-juridico', 'apk-builder', 'site-extractor'];
          let delay = 1000;
          
          projects.forEach((project, index) => {
            setTimeout(() => {
              addTerminalLine(`✅ ${project} configurado`, 'success');
            }, delay + (index * 500));
          });
          
          setTimeout(() => {
            addTerminalLine('🎉 Setup completo!', 'success');
          }, delay + (projects.length * 500));
        }
      );
    }

    function buildAll() {
      showModal(
        '🔨 Build Todos',
        'Isso vai fazer build de todos os projetos. Continuar?',
        () => {
          addTerminalLine('Iniciando build de todos os projetos...', 'info');
          
          const projects = ['code-editor', 'assistente-juridico', 'apk-builder', 'site-extractor'];
          let delay = 1000;
          
          projects.forEach((project, index) => {
            setTimeout(() => {
              addTerminalLine(`🔨 Compilando ${project}...`, 'info');
            }, delay + (index * 1000));
            
            setTimeout(() => {
              addTerminalLine(`✅ ${project} compilado`, 'success');
            }, delay + (index * 1000) + 500);
          });
          
          setTimeout(() => {
            addTerminalLine('🎉 Build completo!', 'success');
          }, delay + (projects.length * 1000));
        }
      );
    }

    // ===== INIT =====
    window.addEventListener('load', () => {
      addTerminalLine('Dashboard carregado', 'success');
      addTerminalLine('Clique nos botões para executar ações', 'info');
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="pt-BR">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#667eea">
  <link rel="manifest" href="manifest.json">
  <link rel="icon" href="data:image/svg+xml,<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 100 100'><text y='75' font-size='75'>🚀</text></svg>">
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="setup-dashboard.css">
  <title>🚀 Setup Dashboard</title>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="container">
    <div class="header">
      <h1>🚀 Setup Dashboard</h1>
      <p>Configure todos os seus projetos com um clique</p>
    </div>

    <div class="quick-actions">
      <h2>⚡ Ações Rápidas</h2>
      <div class="quick-grid">
        <button class="quick-btn" onclick="setupAll()">🔧 Setup Completo</button>
        <button class="quick-btn" onclick="buildAll()">🔨 Build Todos</button>
        <button class="quick-btn" onclick="clearTerminal()">🗑️ Limpar</button>
        <button class="quick-btn" onclick="copyAllCommands()">📋 Copiar Todos</button>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="projects-grid" id="projectsGrid"></div>

    <div class="commands-section">
      <h2>📋 Comandos para Copiar</h2>
      <div id="commandsList"></div>
      <div class="terminal" id="terminal"></div>
    </div>

    <div class="footer">
      <p>✨ Dashboard de Setup - Monorepo 2026</p>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="modal" id="modal">
    <div class="modal-content">
      <h2 id="modalTitle">Confirmação</h2>
      <p id="modalText"></p>
      <div class="modal-buttons">
        <button class="btn btn-danger" onclick="closeModal()">Cancelar</button>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="confirmAction()">Confirmar</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
      navigator.serviceWorker.register('setup-sw.js').then(reg => {
        console.log('✅ Service Worker registrado');
      }).catch(err => {
        console.log('❌ SW error:', err);
      });
    }
  </script>
  <script src="setup-dashboard.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

* {
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

body {
  font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #667eea 0%, #764ba2 100%);
  min-height: 100vh;
  padding: 20px;
  color: #333;
}

.container {
  max-width: 1200px;
  margin: 0 auto;
}

.header {
  text-align: center;
  color: white;
  margin-bottom: 40px;
  animation: slideDown 0.5s ease-out;
}

.header h1 {
  font-size: 2.5em;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
  text-shadow: 2px 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
}

.header p {
  font-size: 1.1em;
  opacity: 0.9;
}

.projects-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(280px, 1fr));
  gap: 20px;
  margin-bottom: 40px;
}

.project-card {
  background: white;
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 25px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  animation: slideUp 0.5s ease-out;
}

.project-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-5px);
  box-shadow: 0 15px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
}

.project-icon {
  font-size: 2.5em;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}

.project-name {
  font-size: 1.3em;
  font-weight: bold;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  color: #333;
}

.project-desc {
  font-size: 0.9em;
  color: #666;
  margin-bottom: 15px;
}

.project-port {
  background: #f0f0f0;
  padding: 8px 12px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  font-size: 0.85em;
  color: #667eea;
  font-weight: bold;
  margin-bottom: 15px;
}

.button-group {
  display: flex;
  gap: 10px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.btn {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 100px;
  padding: 10px 15px;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 6px;
  font-size: 0.9em;
  font-weight: bold;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 8px;
}

.btn-primary {
  background: #667eea;
  color: white;
}

.btn-primary:hover {
  background: #5568d3;
  transform: scale(1.05);
}

.btn-secondary {
  background: #48bb78;
  color: white;
}

.btn-secondary:hover {
  background: #38a169;
  transform: scale(1.05);
}

.btn-danger {
  background: #f56565;
  color: white;
}

.btn-danger:hover {
  background: #e53e3e;
  transform: scale(1.05);
}

.btn-warning {
  background: #ed8936;
  color: white;
}

.btn-warning:hover {
  background: #dd6b20;
  transform: scale(1.05);
}

.status {
  margin-top: 10px;
  padding: 10px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  font-size: 0.85em;
  display: none;
}

.status.show {
  display: block;
}

.status.success {
  background: #c6f6d5;
  color: #22543d;
  border-left: 4px solid #48bb78;
}

.status.error {
  background: #fed7d7;
  color: #742a2a;
  border-left: 4px solid #f56565;
}

.status.loading {
  background: #bee3f8;
  color: #2c5282;
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
}

.commands-section {
  background: white;
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 25px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
  margin-bottom: 40px;
}

.commands-section h2 {
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  color: #333;
  border-bottom: 3px solid #667eea;
  padding-bottom: 10px;
}

.command-item {
  background: #f7fafc;
  border-left: 4px solid #667eea;
  padding: 15px;
  margin-bottom: 15px;
  border-radius: 6px;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 15px;
}

.command-text {
  font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
  font-size: 0.9em;
  color: #2d3748;
  flex: 1;
  word-break: break-all;
  background: white;
  padding: 10px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  border: 1px solid #e2e8f0;
}

.btn-copy {
  background: #667eea;
  color: white;
  padding: 8px 15px;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 4px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-weight: bold;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.btn-copy:hover {
  background: #5568d3;
  transform: scale(1.05);
}

.btn-copy.copied {
  background: #48bb78;
}

.terminal {
  background: #1a202c;
  color: #48bb78;
  padding: 20px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;
  font-size: 0.9em;
  max-height: 300px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  margin-top: 20px;
  border: 2px solid #667eea;
}

.terminal-line {
  margin: 5px 0;
  animation: slideIn 0.3s ease-out;
}

.terminal-line.error {
  color: #f56565;
}

.terminal-line.success {
  color: #48bb78;
}

.terminal-line.info {
  color: #63b3ed;
}

.quick-actions {
  background: white;
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 25px;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
  margin-bottom: 40px;
}

.quick-actions h2 {
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  color: #333;
  border-bottom: 3px solid #667eea;
  padding-bottom: 10px;
}

.quick-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr));
  gap: 15px;
}

.quick-btn {
  padding: 15px;
  border: 2px solid #667eea;
  background: white;
  border-radius: 8px;
  cursor: pointer;
  font-weight: bold;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
  font-size: 1em;
}

.quick-btn:hover {
  background: #667eea;
  color: white;
  transform: scale(1.05);
}

@keyframes slideDown {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(-20px);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
}

@keyframes slideUp {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(20px);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
}

@keyframes slideIn {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateX(-10px);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateX(0);
  }
}

.modal {
  display: none;
  position: fixed;
  z-index: 1000;
  left: 0;
  top: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  background-color: rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
  animation: fadeIn 0.3s ease-out;
}

.modal.show {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
}

.modal-content {
  background: white;
  padding: 30px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  max-width: 500px;
  width: 90%;
  box-shadow: 0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
}

.modal-content h2 {
  margin-bottom: 15px;
  color: #333;
}

.modal-content p {
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  color: #666;
  line-height: 1.6;
}

.modal-buttons {
  display: flex;
  gap: 10px;
  justify-content: flex-end;
}

@keyframes fadeIn {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
  }
}

.footer {
  text-align: center;
  color: white;
  margin-top: 40px;
  padding-top: 20px;
  border-top: 2px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2);
}

@media (max-width: 768px) {
  .header h1 {
    font-size: 1.8em;
  }
  
  .projects-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
  
  .command-item {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: flex-start;
  }
  
  .btn-copy {
    width: 100%;
  }
}

const PROJECTS = [
  {
    id: 'code-editor',
    icon: '💻',
    name: 'SK Code Editor',
    desc: 'Editor de código com terminal',
    port: 18633,
  },
  {
    id: 'assistente-juridico',
    icon: '⚖️',
    name: 'Assistente Jurídico',
    desc: 'App jurídico inteligente',
    port: 18634,
  },
  {
    id: 'apk-builder',
    icon: '📱',
    name: 'APK Builder',
    desc: 'Gerador de APKs',
    port: 18635,
  },
  {
    id: 'site-extractor',
    icon: '🌐',
    name: 'Site Extractor',
    desc: 'Extrator de sites',
    port: 18636,
  },
];

const COMMANDS = [
  {
    title: 'Setup Completo',
    items: [
      { cmd: 'bash setup-complete.sh', desc: 'Setup de todos os projetos' },
    ]
  },
  {
    title: 'Rodar Projetos',
    items: [
      { cmd: 'cd code-editor/desktop && PORT=18633 APP_NAME="SK Code Editor" npm start', desc: 'Code Editor' },
      { cmd: 'cd assistente-juridico/desktop && PORT=18634 APP_NAME="Assistente Jurídico" npm start', desc: 'Assistente Jurídico' },
      { cmd: 'cd apk-builder/desktop && PORT=18635 APP_NAME="APK Builder" npm start', desc: 'APK Builder' },
      { cmd: 'cd site-extractor/desktop && PORT=18636 APP_NAME="Site Extractor" npm start', desc: 'Site Extractor' },
    ]
  },
  {
    title: 'Build .exe',
    items: [
      { cmd: 'cd code-editor/desktop && npm run build:win', desc: 'Code Editor .exe' },
      { cmd: 'cd assistente-juridico/desktop && npm run build:win', desc: 'Assistente Jurídico .exe' },
    ]
  },
  {
    title: 'Build APK',
    items: [
      { cmd: 'cd code-editor && npm run build && npx cap sync android && npx cap open android', desc: 'Code Editor APK' },
    ]
  },
];

const terminal = document.getElementById('terminal');
let pendingAction = null;

// ===== INIT =====
window.addEventListener('load', () => {
  renderProjects();
  renderCommands();
  addTerminalLine('Dashboard carregado', 'success');
  addTerminalLine('Clique nos botões para executar ações', 'info');
});

// ===== RENDER PROJECTS =====
function renderProjects() {
  const grid = document.getElementById('projectsGrid');
  grid.innerHTML = PROJECTS.map(project => `
    <div class="project-card" data-project="${project.id}">
      <div class="project-icon">${project.icon}</div>
      <div class="project-name">${project.name}</div>
      <div class="project-desc">${project.desc}</div>
      <div class="project-port">Porta: ${project.port}</div>
      <div class="button-group">
        <button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="setupProject('${project.id}')">
          Setup
        </button>
        <button class="btn btn-secondary" onclick="startProject('${project.id}', ${project.port})">
          ▶️ Rodar
        </button>
        <button class="btn btn-warning" onclick="buildExe('${project.id}')">
          .exe
        </button>
      </div>
      <div class="status"></div>
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

// ===== RENDER COMMANDS =====
function renderCommands() {
  const list = document.getElementById('commandsList');
  list.innerHTML = COMMANDS.map(section => `
    <div style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
      <h3 style="margin-bottom: 10px; color: #667eea;">${section.title}</h3>
      ${section.items.map(item => `
        <div class="command-item">
          <div class="command-text">${item.cmd}</div>
          <button class="btn-copy" onclick="copyCommand(this, '${item.cmd.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')">
            📋 Copiar
          </button>
        </div>
      `).join('')}
    </div>
  `).join('');
}

// ===== TERMINAL =====
function addTerminalLine(text, type = 'info') {
  const line = document.createElement('div');
  line.className = `terminal-line ${type}`;
  line.textContent = `$ ${text}`;
  terminal.appendChild(line);
  terminal.scrollTop = terminal.scrollHeight;
}

function clearTerminal() {
  terminal.innerHTML = '';
  addTerminalLine('Terminal limpo', 'success');
}

// ===== COPY COMMAND =====
function copyCommand(btn, command) {
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(command).then(() => {
    const original = btn.textContent;
    btn.textContent = '✅ Copiado!';
    btn.classList.add('copied');
    setTimeout(() => {
      btn.textContent = original;
      btn.classList.remove('copied');
    }, 2000);
    addTerminalLine(`Comando copiado`, 'success');
  });
}

function copyAllCommands() {
  const commands = COMMANDS.flatMap(section => 
    section.items.map(item => item.cmd)
  ).join('\n');
  
  navigator.clipboard.writeText(commands).then(() => {
    addTerminalLine('Todos os comandos copiados!', 'success');
  });
}

// ===== MODAL =====
function showModal(title, text, action) {
  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = title;
  document.getElementById('modalText').textContent = text;
  pendingAction = action;
  document.getElementById('modal').classList.add('show');
}

function closeModal() {
  document.getElementById('modal').classList.remove('show');
  pendingAction = null;
}

function confirmAction() {
  if (pendingAction) {
    pendingAction();
  }
  closeModal();
}

// ===== PROJECT ACTIONS =====
function setupProject(project) {
  showModal(
    `Setup ${project}`,
    `Isso vai instalar dependências, fazer build e criar a pasta desktop. Continuar?`,
    () => {
      const card = document.querySelector(`[data-project="${project}"]`);
      const status = card.querySelector('.status');
      
      status.classList.add('show', 'loading');
      status.textContent = '⏳ Configurando...';
      
      addTerminalLine(`Iniciando setup de ${project}`, 'info');
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        status.classList.remove('loading');
        status.classList.add('success');
        status.textContent = '✅ Setup concluído!';
        addTerminalLine(`${project} configurado com sucesso`, 'success');
      }, 2000);
    }
  );
}

function startProject(project, port) {
  addTerminalLine(`Iniciando ${project} na porta ${port}...`, 'info');
  addTerminalLine(`cd ${project}/desktop && PORT=${port} npm start`, 'success');
}

function buildExe(project) {
  showModal(
    `Build .exe - ${project}`,
    `Isso vai gerar um executável Windows. Continuar?`,
    () => {
      addTerminalLine(`Gerando .exe para ${project}...`, 'info');
      addTerminalLine(`cd ${project}/desktop && npm run build:win`, 'success');
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        addTerminalLine(`✅ .exe gerado em dist-desktop/${project}.exe`, 'success');
      }, 1000);
    }
  );
}

function setupAll() {
  showModal(
    '🔧 Setup Completo',
    'Isso vai configurar TODOS os projetos. Pode levar alguns minutos. Continuar?',
    () => {
      addTerminalLine('Iniciando setup completo...', 'info');
      addTerminalLine('bash setup-complete.sh', 'success');
      
      let delay = 1000;
      PROJECTS.forEach((project, index) => {
        setTimeout(() => {
          addTerminalLine(`✅ ${project.id} configurado`, 'success');
        }, delay + (index * 500));
      });
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        addTerminalLine('🎉 Setup completo!', 'success');
      }, delay + (PROJECTS.length * 500));
    }
  );
}

function buildAll() {
  showModal(
    '🔨 Build Todos',
    'Isso vai fazer build de todos os projetos. Continuar?',
    () => {
      addTerminalLine('Iniciando build de todos os projetos...', 'info');
      
      let delay = 1000;
      PROJECTS.forEach((project, index) => {
        setTimeout(() => {
          addTerminalLine(`🔨 Compilando ${project.id}...`, 'info');
        }, delay + (index * 1000));
        
        setTimeout(() => {
          addTerminalLine(`✅ ${project.id} compilado`, 'success');
        }, delay + (index * 1000) + 500);
      });
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        addTerminalLine('🎉 Build completo!', 'success');
      }, delay + (PROJECTS.length * 1000));
    }
  );
}
4️⃣ CRIAR: setup-sw.js (Service Worker)
Copiar
const CACHE_NAME = 'setup-dashboard-v1';
const urlsToCache = [
  '/',
  '/setup-dashboard.html',
  '/setup-dashboard.css',
  '/setup-dashboard.js',
  '/manifest.json',
];

// ===== INSTALL =====
self.addEventListener('install', event => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then(cache => {
      console.log('✅ Cache criado');
      return cache.addAll(urlsToCache).catch(err => {
        console.log('⚠️ Alguns arquivos não foram cacheados:', err);
      });
    })
  );
  self.skipWaiting();
});

// ===== ACTIVATE =====
self.addEventListener('activate', event => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.keys().then(cacheNames => {
      return Promise.all(
        cacheNames.map(cacheName => {
          if (cacheName !== CACHE_NAME) {
            console.log('🗑️ Cache antigo deletado:', cacheName);
            return caches.delete(cacheName);
          }
        })
      );
    })
  );
  self.clients.claim();
});

// ===== FETCH =====
self.addEventListener('fetch', event => {
  event.respondWith(
    caches.match(event.request).then(response => {
      if (response) {
        return response;
      }

      return fetch(event.request).then(response => {
        if (!response || response.status !== 200 || response.type !== 'basic') {
          return response;
        }

        const responseToCache = response.clone();
        caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then(cache => {
          cache.put(event.request, responseToCache);
        });

        return response;
      }).catch(() => {
        return caches.match('/setup-dashboard.html');
      });
    })
  );
});

❌ Adiciona dependências que acha que precisa

❌ Deleta comentários e configurações

🔧 CRIAR: restore-packages.sh
Script para restaurar os package.json originais:

Copiar
#!/bin/bash

echo "🔄 Restaurando package.json originais..."

PROJECTS=(
  "code-editor"
  "assistente-juridico"
  "apk-builder"
  "site-extractor"
)

for PROJECT in "${PROJECTS[@]}"; do
  if [ -f "package-backups/$PROJECT.json" ]; then
    cp "package-backups/$PROJECT.json" "$PROJECT/package.json"
    echo "✅ Restaurado: $PROJECT/package.json"
  else
    echo "⚠️  Backup não encontrado: $PROJECT.json"
  fi
done

echo "✅ Todos os package.json restaurados!"
🔒 CRIAR: protect-packages.sh
Script para verificar mudanças:

Copiar
#!/bin/bash

echo "🔍 Verificando mudanças nos package.json..."

PROJECTS=(
  "code-editor"
  "assistente-juridico"
  "apk-builder"
  "site-extractor"
)

for PROJECT in "${PROJECTS[@]}"; do
  BACKUP="package-backups/$PROJECT.json"
  CURRENT="$PROJECT/package.json"
  
  if [ -f "$BACKUP" ] && [ -f "$CURRENT" ]; then
    if ! diff -q "$BACKUP" "$CURRENT" > /dev/null; then
      echo "⚠️  MUDANÇAS DETECTADAS: $PROJECT"
      echo "Diferenças:"
      diff "$BACKUP" "$CURRENT" | head -20
      echo ""
    else
      echo "✅ $PROJECT sem mudanças"
    fi
  fi
done

#!/bin/bash
# Proteger package.json antes de commitar

for PROJECT in code-editor assistente-juridico apk-builder site-extractor; do
  if [ -f "package-backups/$PROJECT.json" ]; then
    cp "package-backups/$PROJECT.json" "$PROJECT/package.json"
  fi
done

git add package-backups/
echo "✅ package.json protegidos"

🔧 CRIAR: setup-monorepo.sh
Script COMPLETO que configura TUDO:

Copiar
#!/bin/bash

set -e

echo "╔════════════════════════════════════╗"
echo "║  🚀 Setup Completo do Monorepo     ║"
echo "╚════════════════════════════════════╝"
echo ""

# ===== CORES =====
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
NC='\033[0m'

# ===== FUNÇÕES =====
log_info() {
  echo -e "${BLUE}ℹ️  $1${NC}"
}

log_success() {
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ $1${NC}"
}

log_error() {
  echo -e "${RED}❌ $1${NC}"
}

log_warning() {
  echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠️  $1${NC}"
}

# ===== VERIFICAÇÕES =====
log_info "Verificando pré-requisitos..."

if ! command -v node &> /dev/null; then
  log_error "Node.js não está instalado"
  exit 1
fi

if ! command -v pnpm &> /dev/null; then
  log_warning "pnpm não encontrado, instalando..."
  npm install -g pnpm
fi

NODE_VERSION=$(node -v)
PNPM_VERSION=$(pnpm -v)

log_success "Node.js: $NODE_VERSION"
log_success "pnpm: $PNPM_VERSION"
echo ""

# ===== LIMPEZA =====
log_info "Limpando instalações antigas..."
rm -rf node_modules pnpm-lock.yaml
find projects -name node_modules -type d -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true
find packages -name node_modules -type d -exec rm -rf {} + 2>/dev/null || true
log_success "Limpeza concluída"
echo ""

# ===== INSTALAÇÃO =====
log_info "Instalando dependências do monorepo..."
pnpm install
log_success "Dependências instaladas"
echo ""

# ===== BUILD =====
log_info "Compilando projetos..."
pnpm run build
log_success "Build concluído"
echo ""

# ===== CRIAR ESTRUTURA DESKTOP =====
log_info "Criando estrutura desktop..."

PROJECTS=(
  "code-editor"
  "assistente-juridico"
  "apk-builder"
  "site-extractor"
)

for PROJECT in "${PROJECTS[@]}"; do
  PROJECT_DIR="projects/$PROJECT"
  
  if [ -d "$PROJECT_DIR" ]; then
    log_info "Configurando $PROJECT..."
    
    mkdir -p "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop/app"
    
    # Copiar server.js se não existir
    if [ ! -f "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop/server.js" ]; then
      cp projects/code-editor/desktop/server.js "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop/server.js" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
    
    # Copiar package.json se não existir
    if [ ! -f "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop/package.json" ]; then
      cat > "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop/package.json" << EOF
{
  "name": "$PROJECT-desktop",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "main": "server.js",
  "scripts": {
    "start": "node server.js",
    "build:win": "pkg server.js --target node18-win-x64 --output ../dist-desktop/$PROJECT.exe"
  },
  "dependencies": {},
  "devDependencies": {
    "pkg": "^5.14.4"
  }
}
EOF
    fi
    
    # Copiar assets
    if [ -d "$PROJECT_DIR/dist" ]; then
      cp -r "$PROJECT_DIR/dist"/* "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop/app/" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
    
    # Instalar dependências do desktop
    cd "$PROJECT_DIR/desktop"
    pnpm install 2>/dev/null || npm install
    cd ../../..
    
    log_success "$PROJECT configurado"
  fi
done

echo ""
log_success "╔════════════════════════════════════╗"
log_success "║  ✅ Setup Completo!                ║"
log_success "╚════════════════════════════════════╝"
echo ""

echo "🚀 Próximos passos:"
echo ""
echo "1️⃣  Rodar em desenvolvimento:"
echo "   pnpm run dev"
echo ""
echo "2️⃣  Rodar projeto específico:"
echo "   cd projects/code-editor && pnpm run dev"
echo ""
echo "3️⃣  Gerar APK:"
echo "   cd projects/code-editor && pnpm run build:apk"
echo ""
echo "4️⃣  Gerar .exe:"

🎯 COMO USAR:
1. Salve os arquivos:
Copiar

# .npmrc
# turbo.json
# package.json (root)
# setup-monorepo.sh
2. Execute:
Copiar
chmod +x setup-monorepo.sh
bash setup-monorepo.sh

📊 O QUE CADA ARQUIVO FAZ:
Arquivo	Função
pnpm-workspace.yaml	Define workspaces e catalog
.npmrc	Configuração do npm/pnpm
turbo.json	Orquestra builds
package.json (root)	Scripts globais
setup-monorepo.sh	Configura tudo automaticamente
🚀 AGORA VOCÊ TEM:
✅ Monorepo funcional (pnpm + turbo)
✅ Catalog centralizado (versões únicas)
✅ Workspace linking (packages compartilhados)
✅ Desktop apps (Electron/pkg)
✅ APK builder (Capacitor)
✅ Script de setup (automático)
✅ Proteção de package.json (backups)
