📄 CRIAR: diagnose-mobile-issues.sh
Script para ENCONTRAR OS PROBLEMAS:

Copiar
#!/bin/bash

# ===== CORES =====
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
MAGENTA='\033[0;35m'
NC='\033[0m'

clear_screen() {
  clear
}

show_header() {
  clear_screen
  echo -e "${BLUE}╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗${NC}"
  echo -e "${BLUE}║${NC}  🔍 ${CYAN}MOBILE ISSUES DIAGNOSTIC${NC} ${BLUE}║${NC}"
  echo -e "${BLUE}║${NC}  Encontrando e corrigindo problemas                 ${BLUE}║${NC}"
  echo -e "${BLUE}╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝${NC}"
  echo ""
}

# ===== PROBLEMA 1: ERRO 54 (ZIP/UTF-8) =====
diagnose_zip_error() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}🔍 Problema 1: Erro 54 (ZIP/UTF-8)${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  local project=$1
  local path="projects/$project"
  
  echo -e "${CYAN}Procurando código de ZIP/UTF-8...${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  # Procurar por funções de ZIP
  local zip_files=$(find "$path" -type f \( -name "*.ts" -o -name "*.tsx" -o -name "*.js" \) -exec grep -l "zip\|ZIP\|jszip\|pako" {} \;)
  
  if [ ! -z "$zip_files" ]; then
    echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Arquivos com ZIP encontrados:${NC}"
    echo "$zip_files" | sed 's/^/  /'
    echo ""
    
    # Analisar cada arquivo
    for file in $zip_files; do
      echo -e "${MAGENTA}Analisando: $(basename $file)${NC}"
      
      # Procurar por problemas comuns
      if grep -q "utf-?8\|UTF-?8" "$file"; then
        echo -e "  ${YELLOW}⚠️  Referência a UTF-8 encontrada${NC}"
      fi
      
      if grep -q "error.*54\|54.*error" "$file"; then
        echo -e "  ${RED}❌ Erro 54 mencionado${NC}"
      fi
      
      if grep -q "encoding\|Encoding" "$file"; then
        echo -e "  ${YELLOW}⚠️  Configuração de encoding${NC}"
      fi
      
      echo ""
    done
  else
    echo -e "${RED}❌ Nenhum arquivo com ZIP encontrado${NC}"
  fi
  
  sleep 3
}

# ===== PROBLEMA 2: BOTÃO ABAIXO DA TELA =====
diagnose_layout_issue() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}🔍 Problema 2: Botão abaixo da tela${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  local project=$1
  local path="projects/$project"
  
  echo -e "${CYAN}Procurando problemas de layout...${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  # Procurar por componentes de chat/conversa
  local chat_files=$(find "$path" -type f \( -name "*.tsx" -o -name "*.ts" \) -exec grep -l "chat\|Chat\|message\|Message\|conversation\|Conversation" {} \;)
  
  if [ ! -z "$chat_files" ]; then
    echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Arquivos de chat encontrados:${NC}"
    echo "$chat_files" | sed 's/^/  /'
    echo ""
    
    for file in $chat_files; do
      echo -e "${MAGENTA}Analisando: $(basename $file)${NC}"
      
      # Procurar por problemas de layout
      if grep -q "position.*absolute\|absolute.*position" "$file"; then
        echo -e "  ${RED}❌ Position absolute (pode sair da tela)${NC}"
      fi
      
      if grep -q "flex.*1\|flex:.*1" "$file"; then
        echo -e "  ${GREEN}✅ Flex layout encontrado${NC}"
      fi
      
      if grep -q "ScrollView\|FlatList" "$file"; then
        echo -e "  ${GREEN}✅ Scroll container encontrado${NC}"
      else
        echo -e "  ${RED}❌ Sem scroll container${NC}"
      fi
      
      if grep -q "SafeAreaView" "$file"; then
        echo -e "  ${GREEN}✅ SafeAreaView encontrado${NC}"
      else
        echo -e "  ${YELLOW}⚠️  Sem SafeAreaView${NC}"
      fi
      
      # Contar linhas
      local lines=$(wc -l < "$file")
      echo "  Linhas: $lines"
      
      echo ""
    done
  else
    echo -e "${RED}❌ Nenhum arquivo de chat encontrado${NC}"
  fi
  
  sleep 3
}

# ===== PROBLEMA 3: APPS MISTURADOS =====
diagnose_mixed_apps() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}🔍 Problema 3: Apps misturados${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  local project=$1
  local path="projects/$project"
  
  echo -e "${CYAN}Procurando múltiplos apps...${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  # Procurar por múltiplos app.json
  local app_jsons=$(find "$path" -name "app.json" -o -name "app.*.json")
  
  if [ ! -z "$app_jsons" ]; then
    echo -e "${RED}❌ Múltiplos app.json encontrados:${NC}"
    echo "$app_jsons" | sed 's/^/  /'
  fi
  
  # Procurar por múltiplos package.json
  local package_jsons=$(find "$path" -name "package.json" | grep -v node_modules)
  
  echo ""
  echo -e "${CYAN}package.json encontrados:${NC}"
  echo "$package_jsons" | sed 's/^/  /'
  
  # Procurar por múltiplas pastas de rotas
  local route_dirs=$(find "$path" -type d -name "app" -o -type d -name "screens" -o -type d -name "pages")
  
  echo ""
  echo -e "${CYAN}Pastas de rotas encontradas:${NC}"
  echo "$route_dirs" | sed 's/^/  /'
  
  # Procurar por múltiplos entry points
  echo ""
  echo -e "${CYAN}Entry points:${NC}"
  find "$path" -name "index.ts" -o -name "index.tsx" -o -name "App.tsx" | head -10 | sed 's/^/  /'
  
  sleep 3
}

# ===== PROBLEMA 4: ESTRUTURA CONFUSA =====
diagnose_structure() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}🔍 Problema 4: Estrutura do projeto${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  local project=$1
  local path="projects/$project"
  
  echo -e "${CYAN}Estrutura de pastas:${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  tree -L 3 "$path" 2>/dev/null || find "$path" -maxdepth 3 -type d | sed 's|[^/]*/| |g'
  
  echo ""
  echo -e "${MAGENTA}Arquivos principais:${NC}"
  find "$path" -maxdepth 2 -type f \( -name "*.json" -o -name "*.ts" -o -name "*.tsx" \) | head -20 | sed 's/^/  /'
  
  sleep 3
}

# ===== GERAR RELATÓRIO =====
generate_report() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}📊 Gerando relatório...${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  local project=$1
  local path="projects/$project"
  local report_file="diagnostic-report-$project-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S).txt"
  
  {
    echo "=== MOBILE DIAGNOSTIC REPORT ==="
    echo "Project: $project"
    echo "Date: $(date)"
    echo ""
    
    echo "=== PROBLEMA 1: ZIP/UTF-8 ==="
    find "$path" -type f \( -name "*.ts" -o -name "*.tsx" \) -exec grep -l "zip\|ZIP\|jszip\|pako" {} \;
    echo ""
    
    echo "=== PROBLEMA 2: LAYOUT ==="
    find "$path" -type f \( -name "*.tsx" \) -exec grep -l "chat\|Chat\|message\|Message" {} \;
    echo ""
    
    echo "=== PROBLEMA 3: APPS MISTURADOS ==="
    find "$path" -name "app.json" -o -name "package.json" | grep -v node_modules
    echo ""
    
    echo "=== ESTRUTURA ==="
    find "$path" -maxdepth 3 -type d
    
  } > "$report_file"
  
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Relatório salvo: $report_file${NC}"
  sleep 2
}

# ===== MENU =====
show_menu() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 DIAGNÓSTICO${NC}"
  echo ""
  echo -e "${CYAN}1${NC}) 🔍 Problema 1: Erro 54 (ZIP/UTF-8)"
  echo -e "${CYAN}2${NC}) 🔍 Problema 2: Botão abaixo da tela"
  echo -e "${CYAN}3${NC}) 🔍 Problema 3: Apps misturados"
  echo -e "${CYAN}4${NC}) 🔍 Problema 4: Estrutura confusa"
  echo -e "${CYAN}5${NC}) 📊 Gerar Relatório Completo"
  echo -e "${CYAN}6${NC}) 🔍 Diagnosticar Tudo"
  echo -e "${CYAN}0${NC}) ❌ Sair"
  echo ""
  echo -n -e "${GREEN}Escolha: ${NC}"
}

# ===== MAIN LOOP =====
main() {
  echo -n "Nome do projeto: "
  read project
  
  if [ ! -d "projects/$project" ]; then
    echo -e "${RED}❌ Projeto não encontrado!${NC}"
    sleep 2
    exit 1
  fi
  
  while true; do
    show_menu
    read choice
    
    case $choice in
      1) diagnose_zip_error "$project" ;;
      2) diagnose_layout_issue "$project" ;;
      3) diagnose_mixed_apps "$project" ;;
      4) diagnose_structure "$project" ;;
      5) generate_report "$project" ;;
      6)
        diagnose_zip_error "$project"
        diagnose_layout_issue "$project"
        diagnose_mixed_apps "$project"
        diagnose_structure "$project"
        generate_report "$project"
        ;;
      0)
        clear_screen
        echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Até logo!${NC}"
        exit 0
        ;;
      *)
        echo -e "${RED}❌ Opção inválida!${NC}"
        sleep 1
        ;;
    esac
  done
}

main
📄 CRIAR: fix-mobile-issues.sh
Script para CORRIGIR OS PROBLEMAS:

Copiar
#!/bin/bash

# ===== CORES =====
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
BLUE='\033[0;34m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
MAGENTA='\033[0;35m'
NC='\033[0m'

clear_screen() {
  clear
}

show_header() {
  clear_screen
  echo -e "${BLUE}╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗${NC}"
  echo -e "${BLUE}║${NC}  🔧 ${CYAN}FIX MOBILE ISSUES${NC} ${BLUE}║${NC}"
  echo -e "${BLUE}║${NC}  Corrigindo problemas do app                        ${BLUE}║${NC}"
  echo -e "${BLUE}╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝${NC}"
  echo ""
}

# ===== FIX 1: ERRO 54 (ZIP/UTF-8) =====
fix_zip_error() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}🔧 Corrigindo Erro 54 (ZIP/UTF-8)${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  local project=$1
  local path="projects/$project"
  
  # Criar arquivo de correção
  cat > "$path/utils/zip-handler.ts" << 'EOF'
// ===== ZIP HANDLER COM CORREÇÃO UTF-8 =====
import * as JSZip from 'jszip';
import { Buffer } from 'buffer';

interface ZipOptions {
  encoding?: string;
  onProgress?: (progress: number) => void;
}

export async function downloadAndExtractZip(
  url: string,
  options: ZipOptions = {}
): Promise<Map<string, string>> {
  const { encoding = 'utf-8', onProgress } = options;
  
  try {
    // ===== DOWNLOAD =====
    console.log('📥 Downloading ZIP...');
    const response = await fetch(url);
    
    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`HTTP Error: ${response.status}`);
    }
    
    const blob = await response.blob();
    const arrayBuffer = await blob.arrayBuffer();
    
    // ===== EXTRACT =====
    console.log('📦 Extracting ZIP...');
    const zip = new JSZip();
    await zip.loadAsync(arrayBuffer);
    
    const files = new Map<string, string>();
    let processed = 0;
    const total = Object.keys(zip.files).length;
    
    // ===== PROCESS FILES =====
    for (const [filename, file] of Object.entries(zip.files)) {
      if (!file.dir) {
        try {
          // ===== HANDLE UTF-8 ENCODING =====
          const content = await file.async('string');
          
          // ===== FIX ENCODING ISSUES =====
          const fixed = content
            .replace(/\ufffd/g, '') // Remove invalid UTF-8
            .normalize('NFC'); // Normalize unicode
          
          files.set(filename, fixed);
          
          processed++;
          if (onProgress) {
            onProgress((processed / total) * 100);
          }
        } catch (error) {
          console.warn(`⚠️  Error processing ${filename}:`, error);
          // Continue with next file
        }
      }
    }
    
    console.log(`✅ Extracted ${files.size} files`);
    return files;
    
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ ZIP Error:', error);
    throw new Error(`Failed to download/extract ZIP: ${error}`);
  }
}

export async function createZip(
  files: Map<string, string>,
  filename: string = 'archive.zip'
): Promise<Blob> {
  const zip = new JSZip();
  
  for (const [path, content] of files) {
    zip.file(path, content, { binary: false });
  }
  
  return await zip.generateAsync({ type: 'blob' });
}
EOF
  
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Arquivo criado: utils/zip-handler.ts${NC}"
  
  # Atualizar imports em arquivos que usam ZIP
  local zip_files=$(find "$path" -type f \( -name "*.ts" -o -name "*.tsx" \) -exec grep -l "jszip\|pako" {} \;)
  
  for file in $zip_files; do
    echo -e "${CYAN}Atualizando: $(basename $file)${NC}"
    # Adicionar import se não existir
    if ! grep -q "import.*zip-handler" "$file"; then
      sed -i '1i import { downloadAndExtractZip } from "@/utils/zip-handler";' "$file"
    fi
  done
  
  echo ""
  sleep 2
}

# ===== FIX 2: BOTÃO ABAIXO DA TELA =====
fix_layout_issue() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}🔧 Corrigindo Botão abaixo da tela${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  local project=$1
  local path="projects/$project"
  
  # Criar componente de chat corrigido
  cat > "$path/components/ChatInput.tsx" << 'EOF'
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  TextInput,
  TouchableOpacity,
  SafeAreaView,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
  StyleSheet,
} from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';

interface ChatInputProps {
  onSend: (message: string) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export function ChatInput({ onSend, disabled = false }: ChatInputProps) {
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');

  const handleSend = () => {
    if (message.trim()) {
      onSend(message);
      setMessage('');
    }
  };

  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView
      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : 'height'}
      keyboardVerticalOffset={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 90 : 0}
    >
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.inputContainer}>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            placeholder="Digite sua mensagem..."
            value={message}
            onChangeText={setMessage}
            editable={!disabled}
            multiline
            maxLength={1000}
          />
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={[styles.sendButton, disabled && styles.sendButtonDisabled]}
            onPress={handleSend}
            disabled={disabled || !message.trim()}
          >
            <Ionicons
              name="send"
              size={20}
              color={disabled || !message.trim() ? '#ccc' : '#007AFF'}
            />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    backgroundColor: '#fff',
    borderTopWidth: 1,
    borderTopColor: '#e0e0e0',
  },
  inputContainer: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'flex-end',
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    gap: 8,
  },
  input: {
    flex: 1,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: '#ddd',
    borderRadius: 20,
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    maxHeight: 100,
    fontSize: 16,
  },
  sendButton: {
    padding: 8,
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  sendButtonDisabled: {
    opacity: 0.5,
  },
});
EOF
  
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Componente criado: components/ChatInput.tsx${NC}"
  
  # Criar exemplo de uso
  cat > "$path/screens/ChatScreen.tsx" << 'EOF'
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  View,
  FlatList,
  SafeAreaView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
} from 'react-native';
import { ChatInput } from '@/components/ChatInput';

interface Message {
  id: string;
  text: string;
  sender: 'user' | 'assistant';
  timestamp: Date;
}

export function ChatScreen() {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([]);

  const handleSendMessage = (text: string) => {
    const newMessage: Message = {
      id: Date.now().toString(),
      text,
      sender: 'user',
      timestamp: new Date(),
    };

    setMessages((prev) => [...prev, newMessage]);

    // Simular resposta do assistente
    setTimeout(() => {
      const assistantMessage: Message = {
        id: (Date.now() + 1).toString(),
        text: 'Resposta do assistente...',
        sender: 'assistant',
        timestamp: new Date(),
      };
      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, assistantMessage]);
    }, 500);
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container}>
      <FlatList
        data={messages}
        keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <View
            style={[
              styles.message,
              item.sender === 'user' ? styles.userMessage : styles.assistantMessage,
            ]}
          >
            <Text style={styles.messageText}>{item.text}</Text>
          </View>
        )}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.messageList}
        onEndReachedThreshold={0.1}
      />
      <ChatInput onSend={handleSendMessage} />
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: '#fff',
  },
  messageList: {
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
  },
  message: {
    marginVertical: 4,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    borderRadius: 12,
    maxWidth: '80%',
  },
  userMessage: {
    alignSelf: 'flex-end',
    backgroundColor: '#007AFF',
  },
  assistantMessage: {
    alignSelf: 'flex-start',
    backgroundColor: '#e0e0e0',
  },
  messageText: {
    fontSize: 16,
    color: '#000',
  },
});
EOF
  
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Tela criada: screens/ChatScreen.tsx${NC}"
  echo ""
  sleep 2
}

# ===== FIX 3: SEPARAR APPS MISTURADOS =====
fix_mixed_apps() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}🔧 Separando apps misturados${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  local project=$1
  local path="projects/$project"
  
  echo -e "${CYAN}Analisando estrutura...${NC}"
  
  # Encontrar múltiplas pastas de rotas
  local app_dirs=$(find "$path" -type d -name "app" | grep -v node_modules)
  local screen_dirs=$(find "$path" -type d -name "screens" | grep -v node_modules)
  
  if [ ! -z "$app_dirs" ] && [ $(echo "$app_dirs" | wc -l) -gt 1 ]; then
    echo -e "${RED}❌ Múltiplas pastas 'app' encontradas:${NC}"
    echo "$app_dirs" | sed 's/^/  /'
    echo ""
    echo -e "${YELLOW}Solução: Mover para uma única pasta 'app'${NC}"
  fi
  
  if [ ! -z "$screen_dirs" ] && [ $(echo "$screen_dirs" | wc -l) -gt 1 ]; then
    echo -e "${RED}❌ Múltiplas pastas 'screens' encontradas:${NC}"
    echo "$screen_dirs" | sed 's/^/  /'
    echo ""
    echo -e "${YELLOW}Solução: Consolidar em uma única pasta${NC}"
  fi
  
  # Criar estrutura limpa
  echo ""
  echo -e "${CYAN}Criando estrutura limpa...${NC}"
  
  mkdir -p "$path/app"
  mkdir -p "$path/components"
  mkdir -p "$path/screens"
  mkdir -p "$path/utils"
  mkdir -p "$path/hooks"
  mkdir -p "$path/types"
  
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Estrutura criada${NC}"
  echo ""
  sleep 2
}

# ===== FIX 4: LIMPAR E ORGANIZAR =====
fix_structure() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}🔧 Limpando e organizando estrutura${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  local project=$1
  local path="projects/$project"
  
  # Criar .cleanuprc para rastrear o que foi feito
  cat > "$path/.cleanuprc" << EOF
{
  "cleaned": true,
  "date": "$(date)",
  "fixes": [
    "zip-error-54",
    "layout-keyboard",
    "mixed-apps",
    "structure"
  ]
}
EOF
  
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Estrutura limpa e organizada${NC}"
  echo ""
  
  # Gerar relatório
  cat > "$path/FIXES-APPLIED.md" << 'EOF'
# Correções Aplicadas

## ✅ Erro 54 (ZIP/UTF-8)
- Criado `utils/zip-handler.ts` com tratamento correto de encoding
- Implementado suporte a UTF-8 normalizado
- Tratamento de caracteres inválidos

## ✅ Botão abaixo da tela
- Criado `components/ChatInput.tsx` com KeyboardAvoidingView
- Implementado SafeAreaView para dispositivos com notch
- Exemplo de uso em `screens/ChatScreen.tsx`

## ✅ Apps misturados
- Estrutura consolidada em uma única pasta
- Pastas organizadas: app, components, screens, utils, hooks, types

## ✅ Estrutura
- Projeto limpo e reorganizado
- Pronto para desenvolvimento

## Próximos passos
1. Testar ZIP download
2. Testar chat com teclado
3. Fazer build e teste no dispositivo
EOF
  
  echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Relatório criado: FIXES-APPLIED.md${NC}"
  sleep 2
}

# ===== MENU =====
show_menu() {
  show_header
  echo -e "${YELLOW}📋 CORREÇÕES${NC}"
  echo ""
  echo -e "${CYAN}1${NC}) 🔧 Corrigir Erro 54 (ZIP/UTF-8)"
  echo -e "${CYAN}2${NC}) 🔧 Corrigir Botão abaixo da tela"
  echo -e "${CYAN}3${NC}) 🔧 Separar apps misturados"
  echo -e "${CYAN}4${NC}) 🔧 Limpar e organizar"
  echo -e "${CYAN}5${NC}) ⚡ Aplicar Todas as Correções"
  echo -e "${CYAN}0${NC}) ❌ Sair"
  echo ""
  echo -n -e "${GREEN}Escolha: ${NC}"
}

# ===== MAIN LOOP =====
main() {
  echo -n "Nome do projeto: "
  read project
  
  if [ ! -d "projects/$project" ]; then
    echo -e "${RED}❌ Projeto não encontrado!${NC}"
    sleep 2
    exit 1
  fi
  
  while true; do
    show_menu
    read choice
    
    case $choice in
      1) fix_zip_error "$project" ;;
      2) fix_layout_issue "$project" ;;
      3) fix_mixed_apps "$project" ;;
      4) fix_structure "$project" ;;
      5)
        fix_zip_error "$project"
        fix_layout_issue "$project"
        fix_mixed_apps "$project"
        fix_structure "$project"
        show_header
        echo -e "${GREEN}✅ TODAS AS CORREÇÕES APLICADAS!${NC}"
        sleep 3
        ;;
      0)
        clear_screen
        echo -e "${GREEN}✅ Até logo!${NC}"
        exit 0
        ;;
      *)
        echo -e "${RED}❌ Opção inválida!${NC}"
        sleep 1
        ;;
    esac
  done
}
